it did not dispatch.\r\n\r\nThe mandate \"use three lanes\" lived in the prompt and in no gate. EXECUTE and VERIFY left artefacts that\r\nwere checked; PLAN left nothing, so nothing noticed that it had never been dispatched — across every round\r\nto date, `--lane PLAN` had been invoked zero times while the research was written by the session model,\r\nwhich is the EXECUTE lane's model.\r\n\r\nThis gate makes a lane claim falsifiable. Given a dispatch receipt it verifies, fail-closed:\r\n\r\n  lane            the receipt is for the lane the round claims\r\n  served == asked the attempt reports the model that was actually asked for (no silent substitution)\r\n  rank 1          the first attempt is the in-force primary arm, or --allow-rank names another\r\n  ok + usage      the dispatch succeeded and the usage receipt parsed\r\n  nonce           the round's nonce appears in the receipt\r\n  exit 0          the attempt exited cleanly\r\n\r\nExit 0 = the lane was served as claimed. Exit 2 = it was not, with the reasons.\r\nRead-only.\r\n\"\"\"\r\n\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport json\r\nimport pathlib\r\n\r\n\r\ndef judge(receipt: dict, lane: str, nonce: str | None = None, allow_rank: int = 1) -> dict:\r\n    problems: list[str] = []\r\n    attempts = receipt.get(\"attempts\") or []\r\n    if not attempts:\r\n        return {\"verdict\": \"NO_ATTEMPT\", \"lane\": lane, \"problems\": [\"receipt carries no attempt\"]}\r\n    # Which attempt counts? For a finished receipt the attempt that served is the one the dispatcher\r\n    # accepted; for a provisional receipt there is no such mark, so the LAST attempt is the one the run\r\n    # ended on. Judging attempts[0] told a comfortable story: sitting 6's first attempt served\r\n    # gpt-6.1-sol and failed nothing, while the run actually fell through to rank 4 (nvidia deepseek,\r\n    # exit 1). A gate that reports the first attempt as the served arm is the same defect as a summary\r\n    # that only quotes the good part.\r\n    a = attempts[-1]\r\n\r\n    if receipt.get(\"lane\") != lane:\r\n        problems.append(f\"lane_mismatch: receipt={receipt.get('lane')} claimed={lane}\")\r\n    if a.get(\"rank\") != allow_rank:\r\n        problems.append(f\"rank_not_primary: rank={a.get('rank')} allowed={allow_rank}\")\r\n    if a.get(\"exit\") != 0:\r\n        problems.append(f\"dispatch_exit_nonzero: exit={a.get('exit')}\")\r\n    # A provisional receipt is what survives when the dispatcher is torn down before its final write.\r\n    # It can prove WHICH ARM SERVED (lane, rank, provider/model, exit) -- which is the question this gate\r\n    # exists to answer -- and it cannot prove the turn finished cleanly. The distinction is carried in the\r\n    # verdict rather than flattened, and --require-final refuses it outright.\r\n    provisional = receipt.get(\"receipt_complete\") is False\r\n    fallthrough = len(attempts) > 1\r\n    if provisional:\r\n        a_prov = True\r\n        if a.get(\"exit\") != 0:\r\n            problems.append(f\"provisional_attempt_nonzero: exit={a.get('exit')}\")\r\n        if not a.get(\"provider\") or not a.get(\"model\"):\r\n            problems.append(\"provisional_attempt_without_identity\")\r\n    else:\r\n        a_prov = False\r\n        if not receipt.get(\"ok\"):\r\n            problems.append(\"dispatch_not_ok\")\r\n        if not receipt.get(\"usage_receipt_ok\"):\r\n            problems.append(\"usage_receipt_not_ok\")\r\n\r\n    asked = receipt.get(\"used_transport_model\")\r\n    served = a.get(\"model\")\r\n\r\n    def _arm_pair(value, provider_hint=None):\r\n        \"\"\"(provider, model) from either a qualified id or a bare one plus its provider.\r\n\r\n        MEASURED 2026-10-31 18:54: asked `opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash` vs served model `mimo-v2.6-flash`\r\n        (provider `opencode-go`) was reported as `silent_substitution` -- the receipt's own provider field\r\n        said it was the same arm. That accusation is the most damaging one this gate can make, so it must not\r\n        be produced by a string shape: both sides are normalised to (provider, model) before comparing.\r\n        \"\"\"\r\n        if not value:\r\n            return (None, None)\r\n        value = str(value)\r\n        if \"/\" in value:\r\n            head, _, tail = value.partition(\"/\")\r\n Nine FAR runs (036-044) were published with nothing but a ResearchSynthesis.md, and `--lane PLAN`\n+appeared zero times across the engagement. Two explanations were tested and both were wrong:\n+\n+  * \"the lanes are not available\" -- FALSE. Measured this round: PLAN `openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol` served in 12 s,\n+    EXECUTE `opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash` served in 14.46 s, VERIFY_SECURITY `openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol` served\n+    twice. All three work.\n+  * \"the rule is not written down\" -- FALSE. AGENTS.md has carried \"Session plane != lane plane\" since 2026-10-30,\n+    and names this exact failure.\n+\n+The real cause is an incentive gradient: writing the synthesis by hand is ONE step, dispatching three lanes is\n+THREE commands with flags that each have a failure mode (sealed home + OAuth relay, provider-qualified vs bare model\n+id, one work package per brief). Under any time pressure the cheap path wins, and the cheap path leaves prose that\n+looks identical to the routed path.\n+\n+So the fix is not another gate -- gates only refuse after the fact. HG-KSEOS 是什麼\\n\\nHG-KSEOS 是受治理的多閘門本機交付系統：把「使用者目標 → 來源 → 需求 → 設計 → 計畫 → WorkOrder → ExecutionBinding → Profile → Hermes/Codex 實作 → 測試 → 修補 → 獨立驗收 → reducers → local delivery」整條鏈路自動化，並以 immutable evidence、independent checker 與 fail-closed 閘門維持可稽核性。RP-002 v2.0 之後，HG-KSEOS 同時是 **Fabric contract surface 的實際消費者**（F0 policy consumption）。\\n\\n## 2. 目前狀態\\n\\n- **Current claim**: RP-002 v2.0 **Stage-2 externally accepted**（`Fabric\\\\stage\\\\RP002-STAGE-HGK\\\\EXTERNAL_ACCEPTANCE_RECEIPT_STAGE2.yaml`；external verdict `PASS_CHALLENGE`、meta acceptance `GRANTED`）。claim ceiling = `RP002_STAGE2_LOCAL_ACCEPTANCE_PASS_REPAIRED_CANDIDATE` + external PASS。\\n- **Next gate**: `SQP1`（NOT_AUTHORIZED）；Stage-3 contract `NOT_YET_COMPILED`；resume token `RESUME:RP002_STAGE3_SQP1_ENTRY`。**本文件任務不執行 SQP1 / Stage-3。**\\n- **Hermes**: v0.20.0（tag `v2026.8.3`、commit `3c27eb6234bf91b8ceee9e9071591b31e9b148cb`）binding status = fresh-read `config/hermes.json`（current = `ACTIVE_SELECTED_REINSTALL_BASELINE`）；provider `opencode-go`、model `deepseek-v4-flash`。\\n- **HGK HEAD**: `22e21f0340501caf4ff0dbd67663faf49f521b03`（external review bundle 記錄）。\\n- **Named methods**: OpenSpec `CERTIFIED_ACTIVE_BROWNFIELD`；gstack `CERTIFIED_ACTIVE_SELECTED_SLICES`（`S2_ROUTER_EVIDENCE.json`）。\\n- **Production**: `NOT_CLAIMED`（Local Delivery / Stage-2 PASS ≠ Production Authorized）。\\n\\n## 3. Canonical Root\\n\\n`C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS` 是唯一 canonical local root（writable root）。Source/Authority、Shared Spine、RBWI/WP、WorkOrder、AGENTS/SKILLS、Harness/Loop、證據與 reducers 皆在此 root。Obsidian vault、LLM Wiki 產出、RAG/KG 皆為 derived/candidate。\\n\\n`C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\Fabric` = RP-002 的 governance/assurance/interop/knowledge contract surface（policy、contracts、assurance packs、profiles、stage evidence）；HGK 任務以 consumption trace 消費它（§31 F0），Fabric 不建立第二 control plane。\\n\\n## 4. 啟動與健康檢查\\n\\n```text\\npowershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\\\\scripts\\\\doctor.ps1     # blocking 為空且 verdict=PASS 才是健康\\n$env:PYTHONPATH=\\'C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS\\\\src\\'\\n\\'.venv\\\\Scripts\\\\python.exe\\' -m hg_kseos --root \\'C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS\\' doctor   # module form = python -m hg_kseos（已驗證）\\n```\\n\\n## 5. 建立新專案（最少輸入；ordinary-user no-brain entry）\\n\\n```text\\n$env:PYTHONPATH=\\'C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS\\\\src\\'\\n\\'.venv\\\\Scripts\\\\python.exe\\' -m hg_kseos --root \\'C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS\\' project start --goal \"<目標>\" --source <路徑> --target-root <目標目錄>\\n```\\n\\n最少輸入：`goal`、`source_paths`、`target_root`。其餘欄位自動推導；user 正常不需要選擇 lane / Agent / Skill / RAG / KG。\\n\\n## 6. 查看狀態 / Resume / Checkpoint / Stop\\n\\n```text\\n$env:PYTHONPATH=\\'C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS\\\\src\\'\\n\\'.venv\\\\Scripts\\\\python.exe\\' -m hg_kseos --root \\'C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS\\' project status <project_id>\\n\\'.venv\\\\Scripts\\\\python.exe\\' -m hg_kseos --root \\'C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS\\' project resume <project_id>      # 中斷後從 checkpoint 恢復\\n\\'.venv\\\\Scripts\\\\python.exe\\' -m hg_kseos --root \\'C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS\\' project checkpoint <project_id>  # 寫入 normative checkpoint\\n\\'.venv\\\\Scripts\\\\python.exe\\' -m hg_kseos --root \\'C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\HG-KSEOS\\' evolution status                 # evolution 訊號/候選\\n```\\n\\nStop：完成後由 reducers 收尾；attempt budget 耗盡會 `FAILED + STOP_ESCALATE`（不無限遞迴）。rollback 依 WorkOrder `rollback_pointer` / `SUPERSEDED_ARTIFACTS.yaml` 還原，不做整輪重做。\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Part II — 一條龍 Autonomous Project Lifecycle\\n\\n## 7. 流程（RP-002 v2.0 normal lifecycle）\\n\\n```text\\nUser goal / source\\n→ Source Discovery → Source Admission → Intent → Requirements（freeze）→ Design → Plan → TaskSpec\\n→ WorkOrder（writer=codex）→ ExecutionBinding → Profile 分派\\n→ Hermes runtime → Codex implementation → tests → repair → independent acceptance（officer VERIFY_ONLY）\\n→ reducers → local delivery\\n```\\n\\n- **external GPT 不是 normal lifecycle dependency**。\\n- User 不需要手動選 Agent / Skill / RAG / KG / lane。\\n- normal unnecessary HITL = 0。\\n- 高風險 / authority / credential 才 HITL（見 §8）。\\n\\n## 8. Automatic vs HITL + deterministic routing\\n\\n```text\\nTask routing / Memory / RAG / KG / Skill selection / WorkOrder planning\\nrepair / tests / independent checker / candidate evolution     AUTO\\ncredential / destructive action / constitutional / production  HITL\\n```\\n\\n**Lane 選擇是 deterministic 的，user 正常不選**：HGK router 依任務形狀決定（reason code 記入 WorkOrder / evidence）：\\n\\n```text\\nROUTE_DIRECT_ATOMIC      small atomic task → Direct（bounded execution，不強迫 card）\\nROUTE_KANBAN_DURABLE     durable / long persistent objective → Hermes Kanban（project-scoped）\\nROUTE_KANBAN_CROSS_ROLE  cross-role / restart-surviving → Hermes Kanban（dependency graph）\\nROUTE_SWARM_PARALLEL     independent parallel lane → Swarm（on demand）\\nROUTE_CODEX_TRACKED      tracked implementation / narrow repair → Codex\\n```\\n\\nLane ≠ execution method；KANBAN/SWARM/GSTACK/OPENSPEC/CODEX execution surfaces 仍須 raw evidence + independent checker（AGENTS.md default-on）。\\n\\n## 9. Governed Evolution\\n\\n```text\\nobserve → gap → FIT-GAP → candidate → sandbox → tests/negative/security/holdout/NRTV\\n→ independent checker → policy gate → canary / rollback\\n```\\n\\nHuman 角色 = **Authority / Policy Gate**，不是逐步操作員。COV-11-06：promotion 需要 independent verifier + Human Policy Owner（source-resolved）；不存在「LOW risk 一律自動 promote」。`UNBOUNDED_SELF_MODIFICATION=0`；self-evolution 僅限 evidence-backed gap，且需 sandbox / eval / holdout / independent verification / promotion / canary / rollback 完整循環。\\n\\n## 10. Self-Repair（兩層，不重複）\\n\\n- **Hermes tool self-recovery**：terminal retry/truncation readback、write_file on-disk verify、patch already-applied no-op、search near-miss、failure hints、iteration budget。\\n- **HG-KSEOS product-level self-repair**：failed acceptance、requirement mismatch、security regression、architecture defect → repair WorkOrder → 獨立 recheck。\\n- Hermes 負責 runtime 摩擦修復；HG-KSEOS 負責產品規範性修復。\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Part III — Hermes v0.20 Runtime（governed binding）\\n\\n## 11. 身份與所有權\\n\\n- **版本**: `0.20.0` / tag `v2026.8.3` / commit `3c27eb6234bf91b8ceee9e9071591b31e9b148cb`（exact-tag 三者對齊；禁止 floating latest / `hermes update`）。\\n- **Canonical executable**: `C:\\\\Users\\\\user\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\hermes\\\\hermes-agent\\\\venv\\\\Scripts\\\\hermes.exe`（config/hermes.json `HGK-HERMES-BINDING/2`，`ACTIVE_SELECTED_REINSTALL_BASELINE`；`scripts\\\\hermes-core.ps1` PASS）。\\n- **Canonical HERMES_HOME**: `C:\\\\Users\\\\user\\\\AppData\\\\Local\\\\hermes`（Scheme B 單一 runtime）。\\n- **Provider/model**: `opencode-go` / `deepseek-v4-flash`（base `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1`；憑證 `OPENCODE_GO_API_KEY` env-ref，不寫入 evidence）。\\n- **Governed binding / role separation**：Hermes = runtime / orchestrator（Kanban、/goal、SessionDB、checkpoint、tool recovery、compression、approvals）；HGK = normative plane；Hermes 不自行 promote、不當 release authority、其 runtime state ≠ HGK normative state。RP-002 Stage-2 實證：`B1_WORKER.log`（PID 10772）+ board `RP002-FABRIC-BOOTSTRAP` task `t_c69537e5`（F0 trace step 5）。\\n\\n### 11.1 Codex Desktop / Hermes provider lane 隔離\\n\\n- Human-operated Codex Desktop 只使用 `C:\\\\Users\\\\user\\\\.codex`；該設定是受保護的 first-party OpenAI/ChatGPT lane，不得用編輯 `config.toml` 切換到 OpenCodex。\\n- Hermes Codex CLI 只使用 `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\\\HG-KSEOS\\\\codex-hermes` 與 `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\\\HG-KSEOS\\\\opencodex-hermes`。由 `scripts\\\\start-hgk-hermes.ps1` 對自己的 process tree 注入 `CODEX_HOME` / `OPENCODEX_HOME`；禁止 `setx` 或其他全域設定。\\n- 驗證：`powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\\\\scripts\\\\start-hgk-hermes.ps1 -Action Verify`。\\n- 啟動：先以 `-Action StartOpenCodex` 啟動隔離 transport，再以 `-Action Hermes` 啟動 canonical Hermes。缺少 credential、錯誤 home、OpenCodex health failure 都 fail closed，不得 fallback 到 OpenAI。\\n- Rollback：還原或移除本 ChangeSet 新增的 launcher/binding/doc 段落；只在確認沒有 lane process 使用時移除 receipt 列出的 `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\\\HG-KSEOS\\\\codex-hermes`、`opencodex-hermes` 與固定 runtime 副本。不得刪除整個 `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\\\HG-KSEOS`，且不得觸碰 `C:\\\\Users\\\\user\\\\.codex`。\\n\\n責任劃分：\\n\\n```text\\nHG-KSEOS = governance / normative plane（authority、WorkOrder admission、evidence、checker、reducers、release）\\nHermes   = runtime orchestration plane（Kanban、/goal、SessionDB、checkpoint、tool recovery、compression、approvals）\\nCodex    = bounded coding executor\\nOpenCodex = transport（ocx start --port 10100 後 codex 路由才可用）\\nOpenCode Go / DeepSeek = provider / model\\nFabric   = governance/assurance/interop/knowledge contract surface（被 HGK 消費）\\n```\\n\\nRollback baseline：v0.18.2（tag `v2026.7.7.2`、commit `9de9c25f…`）保留為 sealed rollback baseline（RECOVERY_ONLY）。\\n\\n## 12. Kanban 何時用\\n\\n```text\\nsmall atomic task                    → direct/bounded execution（不強迫 card）\\nlong persistent objective            → Hermes /goal\\ndurable / cross-role / restart-surviving → Hermes Kanban（project-scoped）\\nparallel dependent tasks             → Kanban dependency graph\\n```\\n\\n**Hermes runtime state ≠ HGK normative project state**：\\n\\n```text\\nHGK WORKORDERS_ADMITTED → Kanban ready\\nHGK EXECUTING           → Kanban running\\nHGK BLOCKED_HITL        → Kanban blocked + governance reason\\nHGK REPAIRING           → repair/retry task\\nHGK RELEASE_REDUCING    → HGK owns（Kanban 不擁有）\\nHGK DELIVERED           → HGK owns（Kanban 不擁有）\\n```\\n\\nHG-KSEOS WorkOrder → Hermes Kanban 為 thin adapter：Shared Spine 只存 workorder_id / pointer / normative state。RP-002 Stage-2：board `RP002-FABRIC-BOOTSTRAP` 為 project-scoped 實例（B1）。\\n\\n## 13. `/goal` 何時用\\n\\n- long-running WorkOrder → 把 WorkOrder contract 投影成 goal completion contract（outcome / verification / constraints / boundaries / stop_when）。\\n- **`/goal done` ≠ HG-KSEOS PASS**：goal judge 只是 runtime stop helper；final acceptance 仍須 raw evidence → independent checker → reducers → release。\\n\\n## 14. Runtime checkpoint vs Normative checkpoint\\n\\n- **Hermes 保存**: session、goal、worker、Kanban runtime、runtime checkpoint。\\n- **HG-KSEOS 保存**: project normative state、authority refs、WorkOrder IDs、session/Kanban refs、source/evidence hashes、release state、rollback pointer。\\n- 兩者不是鏡像；Shared Spine 只存 pointer/ref。\\n\\n## 15. Tool recovery / Compression / Approvals\\n\\n- Tool self-recovery 由 Hermes v0.20 提供（reuse binding matrix：duplicates=0，HG-KSEOS 無重複 wrapper）。\\n- Context compression 使用 Hermes v0.20（enabled；HG-KSEOS Memory/RAG/SourceUnit 不因 compression 刪除）。\\n- Approvals：`approvals.mode: manual`（fail-closed；不得以 `off` 作正常 profile）；`rm -rf` 等危險 pattern 觸發 `pending_approval`；Windows-native path guard 由 HGK security 層保留。\\n\\n## 16. Skills 治理邊界\\n\\n- Hermes 負責 skill file 機制 / loading / invocation / curator（archive-only，永不 promote）。\\n- HG-KSEOS 負責 candidate admission、FIT-GAP、sandbox、negative/security、holdout、NRTV、independent checker、promotion policy、COV-11-06 Human Policy Owner gate、rollback acceptance。\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Part IV — Named Methods（OpenSpec / gstack）\\n\\n## 17. OpenSpec（brownfield spec projection）\\n\\n- **身份**: `@fission-ai/openspec@1.8.0`（tag `v1.8.0`、release commit `d57889664…`、MIT、Node >=20.19）— `CERTIFIED_ACTIVE_BROWNFIELD`（`S2_ROUTER_EVIDENCE.json`）。\\n- **角色**: `BROWNFIELD_ENGINEERING_DEFINITION_ADAPTER`。不是 Root SSOT、不是 release authority、不是 primary writer（Codex CLI 仍為 bounded writer）。\\n- 使用上游官方 Hermes skills-only 整合（`.hermes/skills/openspec-*/SKILL.md`，`skills.external_dirs` 載入）。\\n- **流程**: brownfield 需求 → deterministic route → OpenSpec explore/propose → spec delta → HGK Requirement/ADR/WorkOrder mapping → Hermes/Codex 實作 → tests → OpenSpec validate/archive → HGK final acceptance。\\n\\n## 18. Spec Kit XOR\\n\\n- `brownfield → OpenSpec active`；`greenfield → Spec Kit/native profile`。禁止雙 spec owner。\\n- tool unavailable → 顯式 `DEGRADED` / native fallback；禁止 silent imitation。\\n\\n## 19. gstack（selected advisory slices）\\n\\n- **身份**: `garrytan/gstack@1.61.0.0`（commit `94993f74012782fd94416dd44b8314f6363a13a4`、MIT、bun）— `CERTIFIED_ACTIVE_SELECTED_SLICES`。\\n- **Selected slices（advisory only）**: `office-hours`、`plan-eng-review`、`review`、`qa`、`cso`、`ship`（`final_authority=false`）、`retro`。\\n- 生成：`bun run gen:skill-docs --host hermes`（54 skills，flat `gstack-*`）；`setup --host hermes` exit 0 ≠ certified（需 effective-load + real invocation）。\\n- 已知 pinned host-generation gap（已記錄 + thin adapter）：`gstack-review` 需要 `.hermes/skills/review/*`，generator 未生成 → adapter 把 repo `review/` 拷入 project `.hermes/skills/review/`。\\n- **邊界**: gstack ship ≠ ReleaseReducer；gstack review ≠ independent checker；gstack cso ≠ HGK security authority；gstack office-hours ≠ product authority。輸出 = EvidenceCandidate / advisory。\\n\\n## 20. Named-Method Router（deterministic）\\n\\n```text\\nbrownfield/migration → openspec\\ngreenfield           → XOR_GREENFIELD / native\\nplan                 → gstack.plan-eng-review（selected）\\nimplementation done  → gstack.review\\nQA required          → gstack.qa\\nsecurity-sensitive   → gstack.cso\\nrelease readiness    → gstack.ship（advisory only）\\nhigh-impact ambiguity→ gstack.office-hours\\nretro                → gstack.retro\\n其餘                 → native\\n```\\n\\n- 決定來自 registry lookup + router（`S2_ROUTER_EVIDENCE.json`），**不是 LLM 猜測**。\\n- **fake invocation = prohibited**；**silent fallback = prohibited**（unavailable → 顯式 DEGRADED + certified native fallback，EvidenceEnvelope 記錄）。\\n\\n## 21. Fallback / Rollback（真實存在的程序）\\n\\n- Hermes v0.20 rollback baseline：v0.18.2（還原 config executable + hermes-core.ps1 驗證）。\\n- OpenSpec disable → native PRW fallback（移除 external_dirs 綁定 + fixture skill projection；canonical Requirement/ADR/WorkOrder 不刪）。\\n- gstack disable → native review/QA/security/release advisory。\\n- Provider failure → route DEGRADED + native fallback。\\n- Checkpoint/resume、package rollback：見 §Part VI 與 scripts（backup/restore-drill）。\\n- RP-002 rollback pointer：`Fabric\\\\stage\\\\RP002-STAGE-HGK\\\\SUPERSEDED_ARTIFACTS.yaml`（被取代工件 preserved unchanged）。\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Part V — Governance & Knowledge\\n\\n## 22. 權威與治理\\n\\n- 權威順序依 source-freeze 工件；低權威來源不得覆寫高權威規則。Fabric contracts（`Fabric\\\\control\\\\*`）經 consumption trace 被消費（F0）。\\n- Canonical database: `var/shared-spine/hg-kseos.db`（單一 writer 契約）。\\n- 同 rank 衝突 → Conflict/TT，不靜默合併。\\n\\n## 23. Knowledge Workbench（Obsidian + LLM Wiki + RAG/KG；KG1 governed）\\n\\n- **Canonical source** = Git Markdown / Source Ledger / Shared Spine / Fabric contracts（frozen）。canonical ≠ RAG/KG/wiki。\\n- **Obsidian / 知識庫** = human workbench（`C:\\\\Projects\\\\Agent_Workspace\\\\知識庫`，derived human view）；**LLM Wiki** = derived compiler（`atomicstrata/llm-wiki-compiler` v1.1.0，npm pin，isolated prefix `var/workbench/llm-wiki`）；**RAG/KG** = retrieval/reasoning surfaces（SQLite/FTS5 local-first）。\\n- **KG1 namespace model**（`Fabric\\\\stage\\\\RP002-STAGE-HGK\\\\KG1\\\\KG1_NAMESPACE_MODEL.json`）：`hgk.candidate.*`（agent write）、`hgk.approved.*`（promotion-gate write）、`hgk.revoked.*`（revocation-gate write + tombstone）、`fabric.*`、`assurance.*`、`sqs.*`（stage2 read-only）、`shared.approved.*`。\\n- **KG1 規則**：agent_write = candidate only；promotion = owner/gate only（agent 禁止 self-promote）；forget = tombstone + audit receipt；revoked/stale excluded；cross-stack export = explicit promotion（`KG1\\\\ACCEPTANCE_RECEIPT_KG1.yaml` runtime evidence：K-001 candidate→approved→revoked + tombstone）。\\n- **KG1 provider state**（`KG1_PROVIDER_LEDGER.json`）：SQLite/FTS5 P0 REUSE（active）、HGK Memory P0 REUSE（candidate ≠ truth）、Qdrant/Neo4j default-off（not-installed）、pgvector conditional、Cognee P1 shadow。\\n- AI 編譯路徑（`llmwiki compile/refresh/query`）需要 ANTHROPIC credential → `EXTERNAL_CREDENTIAL_GATED`。\\n- GraphRAG / vector backend = `DEFERRED_SOURCE_BACKED`（本輪不啟用）。\\n- 編輯流向：filesystem/Git diff → candidate → 驗證/admission → rebuild；不得直接成為 authority。\\n\\n## 24. 安全邊界\\n\\n禁止：寫入來源、越出 WorkOrder admitted write-set / target-root / isolated worktree boundary、路徑穿越、reparse point、未授權網路、秘密讀取、遠端發佈、部署、production mutation。所有高風險擴權維持 HITL。證據不得保存 token/key 值。ContextForge secret policy = env-var `REFERENCE_ONLY`（`__REPLACE_ME__` fail-closed）。\\n\\n---\\n\\n# Part VI — Evidence / Acceptance / Release\\n\\n## 25. 如何判斷 current candidate 是否可交付\\n\\n依序檢查：\\n\\n```text\\nfresh test denominator（current task/head 綁定；schema HGK-CURRENT-TEST-DENOMINATOR/2）\\nCoverageReducer v2 decision\\nUserExperienceReducer v1 decision\\nTST-092 independent receipt（current Task 命名 + same HEAD/package）\\nExternal Acceptance（unique hard-check IDs 全 PASS、duplicates=0）\\nFinal Evidence（single current identity；status/counts 與 acceptance machine 一致）\\nblocking TT = 0\\n```\\n\\n**ad-hoc verification ≠ canonical suite green**：前者是針對變更行為的聚焦檢查，後者是 fresh discovery 的完整套件結果。\\n\\n## 26. Hard-Check denominator 語意\\n\\nExternal Acceptance 的 hard-check denominator 按 **unique predicate ID** 計數；duplicate ID 本身是 fail-closed defect（`hard_check_row_total == hard_check_unique_id_total` 且 `duplicate_hard_check_id_count == 0`）。數量本身不是品質；不保證任何固定數字。\\n\\n## 27. Evidence lineage\\n\\n- Task-011/012/013/014/015 = historical accepted lineage（不可覆寫；TST-092 historical receipts immutable）。\\n- current closure = current candidate only（RP-002 Stage-2 externally accepted 為 current）。\\n- 舊 HEAD/package 保留在 Changelog / Historical Baselines，不得在普通使用章節稱 current。\\n\\n## 28. Package / Release\\n\\n- `scripts\\\\package.ps1`（或 `tools/package_local.py`）產生 deterministic local ZIP + exact-set readback + manifest。\\n- TST-092 以獨立 checker 在 same-HEAD/same-package 上 INDEPENDENT_CASE_PASS。\\n- blocking TT、過期/scope-mismatch evidence、exact-set mismatch → FAIL/TEMP_CLOSED。LLM judge 不能覆寫 deterministic oracle。\\n\\n## 29. 備份 / 還原\\n\\n`scripts\\\\backup.ps1`（不可覆寫 SQLite backup + SHA-256 manifest，`var/backups/`）；`scripts\\\\restore-drill.ps1`（寫入 `worktrees/restore-drill/`，驗證 hash/integrity/schema/counts/RTO）。TT ledger：更新 `source-freeze/TT_LEDGER.csv` 後 `scripts\\\\reconcile-tt.ps1`。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 28a. Canonical acceptance resolver 與 ordering oracle（current）\\n\\n**28a.1 Acceptance transition（唯一入口）**\\n- `SharedSpine.resolve_acceptance(...)` ＋ `TRANSITIONS[\\'acceptance\\']` 是 acceptance 的唯一 canonical transition 入口；由 SharedSpine / domain owner 執行。\\n- 保證：canonical event / audit trace 保留；subject / evidence binding fail-closed；stale / conflicting 拒絕；identical replay idempotent；transaction atomic。\\n- **consumer 不得以直接 SQL bypass domain API**；spine 自身的 repository / transaction 實作可依既有架構使用內部 persistence SQL。\\n- 複核入口：`tests/test_acceptance_resolution.py`。\\n\\n**28a.2 Ordering oracle**\\n- 排序一律用隱式 `rowid`（單調插入序），**不得用 wall-clock `created_at`**（秒級精度，同秒即任意）。\\n- 已修正四處：checkpoint 的 current-workorder 選擇（＝ admission 序）、近期 transitions、memory 記錄、qualified 形式。\\n- 複核入口：`tests/test_ordering_oracle.py`。\\n\\n# Part VII — RP-002 v2.0 現況、Diagnostics & Troubleshooting\\n\\n## 30. \"VERIFY_SECURITY\": (\"VERIFY_RECEIPT.json\", \"openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol\"),\n}\n\n\ndef _gate(receipt: Path, lane: str) -> dict:\n    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(GATE), str(receipt), Path) -> dict:\n    findings, receipts = [], {}\n    for lane, (fname, expect_model) in CASES.items():\n        p = run / fname\n        if not p.exists():\n            findings.append({\"lane\": json.loads(p.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\"))\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n            findings.append({\"lane\": lane, \"problem\": f\"{fname} unreadable: {exc}\"})\n            continue\n        # asked == served, on the pinned arm, and the child echoed the nonce\n        attempts = d.get(\"attempts\") if isinstance(d.get(\"attempts\"), list) else []\n it did not dispatch.\r\n\r\nThe mandate \"use three lanes\" lived in the prompt and in no gate. EXECUTE and VERIFY left artefacts that\r\nwere checked; PLAN left nothing, so nothing noticed that it had never been dispatched — across every round\r\nto date, `--lane PLAN` had been invoked zero times while the research was written by the session model,\r\nwhich is the EXECUTE lane's model.\r\n\r\nThis gate makes a lane claim falsifiable. Given a dispatch receipt it verifies, fail-closed:\r\n\r\n  lane            the receipt is for the lane the round claims\r\n  served == asked the attempt reports the model that was actually asked for (no silent substitution)\r\n  rank 1          the first attempt is the in-force primary arm, or --allow-rank names another\r\n  ok + usage      the dispatch succeeded and the usage receipt parsed\r\n  nonce           the round's nonce appears in the receipt\r\n  exit 0          the attempt exited cleanly\r\n\r\nExit 0 = the lane was served as claimed. A line that fails to parse for\n    any other reason is likewise not a row.\n    \"\"\"\n    rows: list[dict] = []\n    undecodable = 0\n    for _rel, raw in spans:\n        if not raw.strip():\n            continue\n        try:\n            text = raw.decode(\"utf-8\", \"strict\")\n        except UnicodeDecodeError:\n            undecodable += 1\n            continue\n        try:\n            rows.append(strict_json_loads(text))\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n    return rows, undecodable\n\n\ndef usage_rows_since(usage_path: Path, offset: int) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Only the rows APPENDED after byte `offset`, strictly decoded (J1/J2).\n\n    The round-5 checker showed that reading just the last line lets a PREVIOUS run's 200 satisfy\n    the receipt of a call that produced none. Binding to a byte offset captured before the attempt\n    removes that whole class: a stale row is never even considered. H3 (pass-4): a ledger that SHRANK\n    below the captured offset had been rewritten/truncated and has no honest post-attempt region, so\n    the read never resets to zero to recover a stale row.\n\n    Pass-6 J1/J2 narrow this further: an offset that is not a line boundary yields nothing (a\n    continuation is not a row), an unterminated tail yields no row, and a line that fails strict\n    UTF-8 decoding is dropped. `usage_spans_since` + `decode_usage_spans` is the ONE implementation;\n    this thin wrapper keeps the unit surface (`usage_rows_since`) working for callers and tests that\n    only need the decoded rows.\n    \"\"\"\n    rows, _undecodable = decode_usage_spans(usage_spans_since(usage_path, offset))\n    return rows\n\n\ndef usage_prefix_digest(usage_path: Path, offset: int):\n    \"\"\"I1: the sha256 of the ledger's pre-turn bytes `[0:offset)`, or None when unreadable.\n\n    A ledger that GREW past the captured offset can still have had its pre-offset bytes shifted and a\n    stale row copied PAST the offset (the pass-4 shrinking-only check never saw it). Binding the\n    receipt to a DIGEST of the exact pre-turn prefix closes the whole class: at read time the prefix\n    must be byte-identical, so any prepend/shift/replace is detected by CONTENT, not by size.\n\n    A ledger that did not exist before the turn has an EMPTY prefix (`offset == 0`), so the digest is\n    the sha256 of the empty string -- NOT None -- otherwise the ledger's creation during the turn\n    would read as a prefix change and wrongly flag an ordinary first append as a rewrite.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not usage_path.is_file():\n        return hashlib.sha256(b\"\").hexdigest() if offset == 0 else None\n    try:\n        with usage_path.open(\"rb\") as fh:\n            return hashlib.sha256(fh.read(offset)).hexdigest()\n    except Exception:\n        return None\n\n\n_USAGE_ROW_SCALARS = (str, int, float, bool, type(None))\n\n\ndef is_usage_row_shaped(row) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"I2: whether a decoded usage row has the object shape every consumer below requires.\n\n    A row that is not a JSON object (a nonempty array, a bare `true`, a string, an integer) has no\n    `.get`, so the pass-4 parser raised `AttributeError` and the surface exited 1 with NO receipt.\n    The shape is validated HERE, once, so every wrong-shaped row is skipped and COUNTED rather than\n    crashing. `bool`/scalars are excluded deliberately: only a real object can carry the provider/\n    model/status fields a receipt needs.\n    \"\"\"\n    return isinstance(row, dict)\n\n\ndef receipt_reject_reason(entry: dict, usage: dict | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The reason a usage row is NOT this attempt's receipt, or \"\" when it is.\n\n    H2 (pass-4 checker false green): `int(200.9) == 200` accepted a fractional status. The status is\n    now validated STRICTLY as an exact integer equal to 200, with `bool` excluded explicitly (in\n    Python `True` is an `int` equal to 1, so a JSON `true` must never read as a status). Floats,\n    strings and every other shape are rejected, and the reason is recorded rather than swallowed.\n\n    The relay records its OWN account-scoped provider id (the openai arm reports openai-p6a1fe3), so\n    the provider comparison is one explicit alias rule while model and the 200 stay exact.\n\n    J3 (pass-6, exact typing): the model and provider are compared on EXACT types and values. A `str`\n    field equals only a `str`; a JSON integer `7` is NOT the string `\"7\"`. The old\n    `str(usage.get(\"model\", \"\")) != str(entry[\"model\"])` coerced BOTH sides through `str()`, so a\n    declared string model `\"7\"` accepted a usage row whose model was the integer `7`. Every field is\n    now validated for shape before it is compared, and a type mismatch is a RECORDED rejection (never\n    a match). The provider alias (`openai-codex` accepts an `openai*` relay id) applies only when\n    BOTH sides are `str`, so a non-string provider can never alias into a match.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not usage:\n        return \"no usage row appended after the attempt offset\"\n    status = usage.get(\"status\")\n    if isinstance(status, bool) or not isinstance(status, int):\n        return (f\"usage status is not an exact integer: \"\n                f\"{type(status).__name__}={status!r}; only the integer 200 is accepted\")\n    if status != 200:\n        return f\"usage status is not 200: {status!r}\"\n    # --- model: exact type AND value (J3) -------------------------------------------------------\n    want_model = entry.get(\"model\")\n    got_model = usage.get(\"model\")\n    if not isinstance(want_model, str):\n        # The pool entry itself is malformed; refuse rather than coerce it into a match.\n        return f\"declared model is not a string: {type(want_model).__name__}={want_model!r}\"\n    if not isinstance(got_model, str):\n        return (f\"usage model is not a string: {type(got_model).__name__}={got_model!r}; \"\n                f\"a non-string can never equal {want_model!r}\")\n    if got_model != want_model:\n        return f\"usage model mismatch: {got_model!r} is not {want_model!r}\"\n    # --- provider: exact string comparison plus ONE explicit alias rule (J3) --------------------\n    want_prov = entry.get(\"provider\")\n    got_prov = usage.get(\"provider\")\n    if not isinstance(want_prov, str):\n        return f\"declared provider is not a string: {type(want_prov).__name__}={want_prov!r}\"\n    if not isinstance(got_prov, str):\n        return (f\"usage provider is not a string: {type(got_prov).__name__}={got_prov!r}; \"\n                f\"a non-string can never equal {want_prov!r}\")\n    if want_prov == got_prov:\n        return \"\"\n    if want_prov == \"openai-codex\" and got_prov.startswith(\"openai\"):\n        return \"\"\n    return f\"usage provider mismatch: {got_prov!r} is not {want_prov!r}\"\n\n\ndef receipt_matches(entry: dict, usage: dict | None) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True only when `receipt_reject_reason` finds nothing to reject. A single decision point, so\n    the verdict and its recorded reason can never disagree.\"\"\"\n    return receipt_reject_reason(entry, usage) == \"\"\n\n\ndef carrier_line_texts(raw_bytes) -> tuple[list[str], int]:\n    \"\"\"S3 (REQ-STRICT-DECLARATION-014): split the captured stream into COMPLETE carrier lines, as BYTES\n    split on `b\"\\\\n\"` only, and return `(texts, undecodable_count)`.\n\n    The wrapper normalizes the argument to a byte stream: an already-captured byte string is split on\n    `b\"\\\\n\"`; a non-UTF-8-decodable TEXT (a surrogateescape decode) is recovered to its original bytes\n    via `surrogateescape`, so the byte-level rule is applied identically whether the caller passes the\n    preserved bytes or the surrogate text.\n\n    A carrier counts as evidence only when it is a COMPLETE, LF/CRLF-terminated, strictly\n    UTF-8-decodable JSON value read in full:\n      * iterate as BYTES split on `b\"\\\\n\"` — never `str.splitlines()`, which also splits on U+2028,\n        U+2029, `\\\\x85`, `\\\\x0b` and `\\\\x0c` and so invents line boundaries the NDJSON protocol has not;\n      * a trailing fragment with NO terminator is NOT a row;\n      * each line is decoded with `errors=\"strict\"`: a line containing an invalid byte is NOT a row and\n        is COUNTED (`carrier_lines_undecodable`, returned as the second element), never turned into a\n        surrogate or U+FFFD;\n      * the line's bytes are EXACTLY the bytes between the terminators (T1, pass 2): the ONLY thing\n        removed is at most ONE trailing `\\\\r`, so a CRLF stream still parses. There is NO unrestricted\n        pre-cleaning strip: a leading NEL (U+0085), a trailing U+2028, a trailing vertical tab\n        (U+000B) or any other non-JSON-whitespace character reaches the parser unchanged and makes it\n        fail -- which is the correct outcome, because those are not JSON whitespace. JSON defines its\n        own insignificant whitespace (space, tab, LF, CR) and the PARSER decides, not a sanitizer.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(raw_bytes, (bytes, bytearray)):\n        blob = bytes(raw_bytes)\n    elif isinstance(raw_bytes, str):\n        # a `surrogateescape` decode round-trips to the exact original bytes (including a lone surrogate\n        # for 0xFF); a strict-decodable text decodes back to the same bytes.\n        blob = raw_bytes.encode(\"utf-8\", \"surrogateescape\")\n    else:\n        raise TypeError(f\"carrier stream must be bytes or str, not {type(raw_bytes).__name__}\")\n    texts: list[str] = []\n    undecodable = 0\n    n = len(blob)\n    start = 0\n    while start < n:\n        nl = blob.find(b\"\\n\", start)\n        if nl == -1:\n            break                          # an unterminated trailing fragment is NOT a row (S3)\n        line = blob[start:nl]\n        if line.endswith(b\"\\r\"):\n            line = line[:-1]               # strip ONE trailing CR so a CRLF stream still parses (T1)\n        start = nl + 1\n        # T1 (pass 2): NO `line.strip()`. The line is returned verbatim (minus at most one trailing CR);\n        # an empty line yielded no text and is not a row, but that is decided by DECODING it to \"\" and\n        # letting the caller's parser reject it, NOT by a pre-clean that deletes real characters.\n        try:\n            text = line.decode(\"utf-8\", \"strict\")\n        except UnicodeDecodeError:\n            undecodable += 1               # not decodable -> NOT a row, counted, never substituted\n            continue\n        if text == \"\":\n            continue                       # an empty line is not a row; skipped without any strip()\n        texts.append(text)\n    return texts, undecodable\n\n\ndef agent_message_texts(out) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"G1/H5/S3: extract the assistant-message TEXT from every `item.completed`/`agent_message` carrier,\n    KEEPING ONLY JSON STRING values, and guarding every carrier access so a wrongly-shaped carrier\n    yields no text instead of an exception.\n\n    The stream is read by `carrier_line_texts` (S3): bytes split on `b\"\\\\n\"`, terminated-only, strictly\n    UTF-8-decoded. A line that does not decode is dropped (never a surrogate/U+FFFD). `json.loads` is\n    then applied to the decoded text. Pass-2 repairs land here:\n      * T1: the decoded line is used EXACTLY as `carrier_line_texts` returned it -- there is NO\n        `.strip()` pre-clean, so a leading NEL / trailing U+2028 / trailing U+000B reaches the parser\n        and makes it fail. A blank or whitespace-only line simply does not start with `{` and yields\n        no event, which is the parser's own decision, not a sanitizer's.\n      * T2: the parse is `strict_json_loads`, so a carrier carrying the non-JSON constants\n        `NaN`/`Infinity`/`-Infinity` RAISES and yields no event (never a permissive float).\n      * T3: a parsed `text` carrying a surrogate code point (U+D800-U+DFFF), e.g. an escaped ``\\\\ud800``,\n        is SKIPPED and COUNTED (`carrier_lines_surrogate`) -- a surrogate can never be searched as if\n        it were the arm's message.\n    Two pass-4 constraints still hold here:\n      * no lossy decode MANUFACTURES a character into the searchable text; and\n      * a truthy non-object `item` (e.g. JSON `true`/`5`/`\"x\"`/a list) made `item.get(...)` raise\n        `AttributeError` - a fail-closed crash with no typed receipt (H5).\n    Every value is validated for shape before it is read; a non-string `text`, or a carrier whose\n    `item` is not an object, contributes nothing.\n    \"\"\"\n    lines, _undecodable = carrier_line_texts(out)\n    texts: list[str] = []\n    for line in lines:\n        if not line.startswith(\"{\"):\n            continue\n        try:\n            event = strict_json_loads(line)\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n        if not isinstance(event, dict) or event.get(\"type\") != \"item.completed\":\n            continue\n        item = event.get(\"item\")\n        if not isinstance(item, dict):\n            continue                      # H5: wrong-shaped carrier -> no text, no crash\n        text = item.get(\"text\")\n        if item.get(\"type\") == \"agent_message\" and isinstance(text, str):\n            if has_surrogate(text):\n                # T3: text carrying a surrogate code point never decides a verdict; skip and count it.\n                _CARRIER_SURROGATE_COUNT[0] += 1\n                continue\n            texts.append(text)\n    return texts\n\n\n# T3 (pass 2): a process-local counter of carrier texts SKIPPED for carrying a surrogate code point.\n# `agent_message_texts` increments it; a caller (run_candidate) reads it into the attempt receipt so a\n# surrogate-bearing carrier is operator-visible and can never be a silent no-op.\n_CARRIER_SURROGATE_COUNT = [0]\n\n\ndef reset_carrier_surrogate_count() -> None:\n    _CARRIER_SURROGATE_COUNT[0] = 0\n\n\ndef carrier_surrogate_count() -> int:\n    return _CARRIER_SURROGATE_COUNT[0]\n\n\ndef nonce_in_agent_message(out: str, nonce: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"The nonce must appear in an ASSISTANT message item - not merely anywhere in stdout.\n\n    MEASURED (aborted first AO attempt): a plain `nonce in stdout` check returned true on a run that\n    exited 1 with zero artifacts, i.e. the old check could be satisfied WITHOUT a successful turn.\n    The exact carrier of that string was not captured (the killed run's stdout was lost), so no\n    mechanism is asserted here; what is asserted is that the nonce is now read only out of an\n    `item.completed` / `agent_message` item, i.e. a model-produced message (N1: a non-conforming\n    answer is refused even though the usage receipt matched; N2: the happy path still passes).\n\n    G1 (pass-3 checker false green): the searchable text may be used ONLY when it is a JSON STRING.\n    The old code did `str(item.get(\"text\", \"\"))`, so a JSON `null` became the string \"None\" and - with\n    `--nonce None` - a message the child never emitted \"satisfied\" the nonce. The value \"None\" is not\n    the defect; the coercion is. Any non-string shape (null, number, bool, object, array) yields NO\n    text, and the substring test is never run against a coerced rendering.\n    \"\"\"\n    return any(nonce in text for text in agent_message_texts(out))\n\n\ndef front_door_port(relay_base: str):\n    \"\"\"The door port when the dispatch is routed through a front door, else None (direct to the relay).\n\n    Deliberately NOT a hardcoded port list: any port other than the relay's own 10101 is a door, so\n    the field cannot silently misreport when a new door is introduced (it did: this said False while\n    the turn went through 10105).\n    \"\"\"\n    tail = str(relay_base).split(\"//\", 1)[-1]\n    host = tail.split(\"/\", 1)[0]\n    port = host.rsplit(\":\", 1)[-1] if \":\" in host else \"\"\n    return port if port.isdigit() and port != \"10101\" else None\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Identity / health gate + non-mutation guard  (FAR-AO-INDEPENDENCE-20261010)\n#\n# MEASURED root cause of \"the AO step is not a real independent check\" (round FAR-OAUTH-WINDOW-20261010):\n#   1. the dispatch kept only a 400-char stdout TAIL, so an AO verdict that did not land in the tail\n#      was unrecoverable (receipt E3: ADVISORY_INCONCLUSIVE_FROM_RECORD);\n#   2. the checker ran under a sandbox that refuses the model's own shell commands on this host, so\n#      it could not re-derive anything and could only read what it was handed.\n# A third defect is NOT a harness bug and is not fixed by code: all three lanes resolve to the same\n# model, so maker==checker is possible by construction. That is recorded, not silently improved.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nIDENTITY_NONE = \"none\"          # key-auth arm: the relay holds the key; no human-attended identity\nIDENTITY_CLI_LOGIN = \"cli_login\"    # the arm needs a CLI login in ITS OWN home\nIDENTITY_RELAY_POOL = \"relay_pool\"  # the arm consumes the relay's ChatGPT subscription credential\n\nERR_IDENTITY_NOT_PREAUTHORIZED = \"ERR_LANE_IDENTITY_NOT_PREAUTHORIZED\"\nERR_RELAY_OAUTH_NEEDS_REAUTH = \"ERR_RELAY_OAUTH_NEEDS_REAUTH\"\nERR_CHECKER_MUTATED_SUBJECT = \"ERR_CHECKER_MUTATED_SUBJECT\"\n# G3 (pass-3 checker false green): a guard root that RESOLVES but holds ZERO files proves nothing,\n# yet it produced exit 0 / ok:true / mutated:false over a digest of []. This gate is the SECOND layer, and it is the\n# only layer that lives on the REAL lane path: `scripts/lane_launcher.sh` is invoked ONLY by the\n# throwaway test harness `tests/w3_solution/lane_harness.py` (stub codex binaries, no production\n# caller), so a control wired only there is DETECTION_ONLY. `run_candidate` is where a lane\n# actually spawns (`subprocess.run` -> codex), so the control is placed here.\n#\n# The policy is SINGLE-SOURCED in the sibling gate `scripts/gcm-containment/lane_credential_posture.py`\n# (itself built on the canonical classifier in `credential_preflight.py`). This file CONSUMES that\n# gate's decision rather than re-deriving a second, weaker check: `declared_credential_posture_decision()`\n# states whether the asymmetric policy is a provable function of the preflight verdict, and\n# `run_candidate` falls back to that exact asymmetric mapping only if a deployed gate disagrees. The\n# mapping is deliberately ASYMMETRIC and is stated, not hidden:\n#   * FAIL (the effective resolution POSITIVELY reaches a prompt-capable interactive helper)\n#     -> BLOCK. A lane runs unattended; if the helper can open a window, the run hangs with nobody\n#     watching. Fail-closed on the danger is the whole point.\n#   * INCONCLUSIVE (the detector could NOT reach a verdict - e.g. git could not be executed)\n#     -> WARN on stderr with a typed code, then CONTINUE. Blocking here would turn a detector bug\n#     into a lane outage, which violates the owner's low-resistance criterion; L1 still holds\n#     structurally either way.\n#   * PASS -> continue silently.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nERR_CREDENTIAL_POSTURE_UNCONTAINED = \"ERR_CREDENTIAL_POSTURE_UNCONTAINED\"\nDEFAULT_CREDENTIAL_POSTURE_GATE = ROOT / \"scripts\" / \"gcm-containment\" / \"lane_credential_posture.py\"\n\n\ndef declared_credential_posture_decision(verdict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The declared asymmetric policy, single-sourced so a deployed gate cannot silently drift.\n\n    Returns ``\"BLOCK\"`` / ``\"WARN_CONTINUE\"`` / ``\"CONTINUE\"`` for a gate-provided verdict. An\n    UNKNOWN verdict maps to ``\"WARN_CONTINUE\"`` -- the fail-closed direction for the DETECTOR is to\n    continue and warn, never to block a lane on a detector fault, and never to treat an unrecognised\n    verdict as a pass.\n    \"\"\"\n    if verdict == \"FAIL\":\n        return \"BLOCK\"\n    if verdict == \"PASS\":\n        return \"CONTINUE\"\n    return \"WARN_CONTINUE\"\n\n\ndef run_credential_posture_gate(gate_path=None, git_path=None, test_global=None) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Run the side-effect-free GCM posture gate and return its raw decision document.\n\n    The gate is executed as its OWN process (its own exit status, its own json) so nothing it reads\n    leaks back into this process. NEVER raises for a detector fault: a missing script, an unparsable\n    output or an unexpected exit is reported as INCONCLUSIVE with the WARN_CONTINUE decision, which\n    the caller warns on and continues past. `git_path` / `test_global` are operator/test seams passed\n    straight through to the gate's own `--git` / `--test-global` flags so the three-valued policy can\n    be driven deterministically against a scratch posture with NO credential window; leaving them\n    unset is the production posture (the gate auto-detects git and reads the real scopes).\n    \"\"\"\n    script = Path(gate_path or DEFAULT_CREDENTIAL_POSTURE_GATE)\n    if not script.is_file():\n        return {\"verdict\": \"INCONCLUSIVE\", \"decision\": \"WARN_CONTINUE\",\n                \"reason\": f\"posture gate not found: {script}\"}\n    argv = [sys.executable, str(script), \"--json\"]\n    if git_path:\n        argv += [\"--git\", str(git_path)]\n    if test_global:\n        argv += [\"--test-global\", str(test_global)]\n    try:\n        proc = subprocess.run(argv, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding=\"utf-8\",\n                              errors=\"replace\", stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=60)\n    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:\n        return {\"verdict\": \"INCONCLUSIVE\", \"decision\": \"WARN_CONTINUE\",\n                \"reason\": f\"could not run the posture gate ({type(exc).__name__}): {exc}\"}\n    # Capture/propagate the gate's OWN stderr verbatim so an INCONCLUSIVE detector fault is\n    # operator-visible even when run_candidate is exercised inside a captured process (a test\n    # harness, a relay). This is a pure pass-through: no policy is re-derived here.\n    if proc.stderr:\n        sys.stderr.write(proc.stderr)\n    try:\n        result = strict_json_loads(proc.stdout)\n    except Exception as exc:\n        return {\"verdict\": \"INCONCLUSIVE\", \"decision\": \"WARN_CONTINUE\",\n                \"reason\": f\"posture gate output was not JSON ({type(exc).__name__}); rc={proc.returncode}\"}\n    if not isinstance(result, dict) or result.get(\"verdict\") not in (\"PASS\", \"FAIL\", \"INCONCLUSIVE\"):\n        return {\"verdict\": \"INCONCLUSIVE\", \"decision\": \"WARN_CONTINUE\",\n                \"reason\": f\"posture gate returned no usable verdict (rc={proc.returncode})\"}\n    # Declared policy: the decision is a pure function of the verdict. If a deployed gate disagrees\n    # with the declared mapping, DEFER to the declared mapping -- the dispatch path owns the policy\n    # and must not be silently overridden by a drifted gate.\n    result[\"declared_decision\"] = declared_credential_posture_decision(result.get(\"verdict\"))\n    if result.get(\"decision\") not in (None, result[\"declared_decision\"]):\n        result[\"decision_disagreement\"] = {\"gate\": result.get(\"decision\"),\n                                           \"declared\": result[\"declared_decision\"]}\n        result[\"decision\"] = result[\"declared_decision\"]\n    return result\n\n\nclass IdentityRefused(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Typed, fail-closed pre-spawn refusal. Carries the code so a caller can act without parsing.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, code: str, detail: str):\n        super().__init__(f\"{code}: {detail}\")\n        self.code = code\n        self.detail = detail\n\n\nclass CredentialPostureRefused(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Typed, fail-closed pre-spawn refusal for a POSITIVELY uncontained credential posture.\n\n    Raised from `run_candidate` and caught in `main()`: `run_candidate` is contractually an attempt\n    RECORD, so a policy refusal must never masquerade as one (that would both corrupt the attempt\n    list and misreport the posture as a model/usage result). Carries the raw preflight verdict so the\n    refusal receipt can quote the offending helper and its origin.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, code: str, detail: str, posture: dict):\n        super().__init__(f\"{code}: {detail}\")\n        self.code = code\n        self.detail = detail\n        self.posture = posture\n\n\ndef default_identity_requirement(entry: dict, lane: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Which identity this arm actually needs when the pool artifact does not declare one.\n\n    MEASURED, and the reason an earlier draft was wrong: an arm routed through the relay with\n    `-c openai_base_url=` consumes the RELAY's credential, NOT the lane home's CLI login. The lane\n    home being `Not logged in` is therefore not a reason to refuse an `openai` arm - only a lane\n    that has to reach OpenAI itself needs `cli_login`.\n    \"\"\"\n    declared = entry.get(\"identity_requirement\")\n    if declared:\n        return str(declared)\n    return IDENTITY_RELAY_POOL if entry.get(\"provider\") in (\"openai-codex\", \"openai\") else IDENTITY_NONE\n\n\ndef sha256_file(path) -> str:\n    h = hashlib.sha256()\n    with open(path, \"rb\") as fh:\n        for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 20), b\"\"):\n            h.update(chunk)\n    return h.hexdigest()\n\n\ndef emit_refusal(args, rec: dict) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Print a typed refusal and persist it. Exists so every refusal path returns the same shape.\"\"\"\n    # FIX 3 (C3): every refusal receipt records the resolved home and whether it is the declared\n    # lane home. DETECTION_ONLY on the DECLARED dispatch path.\n#\n# This is a PRE-SPAWN check of the lane BRIEF for remote-write markers. It CANNOT stop a lane that\n# calls `git push` from inside its own shell once spawned; it only refuses to *dispatch* a lane\n# whose brief text carries the markers without an attached publication WorkOrder reference. Do not\n# describe it as preventing unintended pushes; it is a detection surface on the declared path.\n#\n# It is SEPARATE from the credential-posture control: \"no prompt\" (a hang risk) and \"no unintended\n# push\" (a write-authorization risk) are different failure modes, and closing the first does not\n# close the second.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nREMOTE_WRITE_MARKERS = (\n    \"git push\", \"--force\", \"force-with-lease\", \"publish\", \"refs/heads\",\n)\n# D6 (round HGK-GCM-REPAIR2-20261013): the alternation captures the BARE WorkOrder id for the\n# labelled forms (`WorkOrder: WO-PUB-9` -> group(1) `WO-PUB-9`; `PUBLICATION WO: X-1` -> group(2)\n# `X-1`). A bare `WO-...` token matches group(0) with no label to strip. `detect_publication_workorder`\n# returns the id WITHOUT the label prefix; round 1 returned group(0) (\"WorkOrder: WO-PUB-9\") and\n# relaxed the test to `assertIn` instead of fixing the extraction (D6/D7).\nPUBLICATION_WO_RE = re.compile(\n    # A labelled id must LOOK like an id (contains a digit) so ordinary prose (\"no workorder here\")\n    # is not mistaken for an authorization (D6 boundary).\n    r\"(?:WO-[A-Za-z0-9-]+\"\n    r\"|WorkOrder[:\\s#-]*([A-Za-z]+-[A-Za-z0-9-]+|[A-Za-z]*[0-9][A-Za-z0-9]*)\"\n    r\"|PUBLICATION[_ -]?WO[:\\s#-]*([A-Za-z]+-[A-Za-z0-9-]+|[A-Za-z]*[0-9][A-Za-z0-9]*))\",\n    re.IGNORECASE)\n\n\ndef _read_text(path) -> str:\n    try:\n        return Path(path).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n    except OSError:\n        return \"\"\n\n\ndef detect_remote_write_markers(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"The remote-write markers present in `text`, in canonical order (case-insensitive, verbatim).\"\"\"\n    low = (text or \"\").lower()\n    return [m for m in REMOTE_WRITE_MARKERS if m.lower() in low]\n\n\ndef detect_publication_workorder(text: str) -> str | None:\n    m = PUBLICATION_WO_RE.search(text or \"\")\n    if not m:\n        return None\n    # Return the BARE id: group(0) for a bare `WO-...` token; the first populated capture group for\n    # the labelled `WorkOrder`/`PUBLICATION WO` forms.\n    for g in (1, 2):\n        if m.group(g):\n            return m.group(g)\n    return m.group(0)\n\n\ndef remote_write_precheck(*, brief_text: str, publication_wo: str | None) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Decide whether a DETECTION_ONLY remote-write refusal applies for this lane dispatch.\n\n    Returns ``{\"markers\": [...], \"publication_wo\": ..., \"would_refuse\": bool}``. Decoding with errors=\"replace\" and\n        re-encoding cannot be byte-exact (a non-UTF-8 byte becomes U+FFFD, then 3 bytes), so\n        \"byte-identical\" would have been an over-claim.\n      * the run id is part of the filename. `rglob` raises FileNotFoundError (WinError 3) as soon as one directory\n    exceeds MAX_PATH, and a crash here aborts the whole dispatch AFTER the pre-spawn gates passed.\n    Measured 2026-10-30 on `var/hgk-solution-apply-20261012/.../references` — the same class of\n    defect as the truncated manifest walk, so the walk goes through the extended-length prefix and\n    unreadable entries are skipped by name rather than taking the turn down.\n    \"\"\"\n    snap: dict[str, str] = {}\n    pfx = (chr(92) * 2 + \"?\" + chr(92)) if os.name == \"nt\" else \"\"\n    for root in roots or []:\n        r = Path(root)\n        if r.is_file():\n            snap[str(r).replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\")] = sha256_file(r)\n        elif r.is_dir():\n            base = pfx + str(r.resolve())\n            for diff_subject(before: dict, after: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"removed/changed VOID the verdict. `added` is recorded separately: a checker may legitimately\n    write its OWN verdict file, and the subject roots are chosen to exclude that.\n\n    H4 (pass-4 checker): an empty side of the snapshot proves nothing, yet `diff_subject({}, {})`\n    returned `mutated: false` with no marker - a clean verdict produced from nothing. Whenever EITHER\n    side is empty the comparison is INCONCLUSIVE, and `mutated` is NOT a clean `false`.\n    \"\"\"\n    added = sorted(k for k in after if k not in before)\n    removed = sorted(k for k in before if k not in after)\n    changed = sorted(k for k in after if k in before and after[k] != before[k])\n    mutated = bool(removed or changed)\n    empty_side = (not before) or (not after)\n    # A measured removal/change still VOIDS even when it empties the subject: the removal IS the\n    # evidence. INCONCLUSIVE is reserved for a comparison that measured NOTHING (empty side, no\n    # removed/changed), so a clean verdict can never be produced from nothing.\n    verdict = \"VOID\" if mutated else (\"INCONCLUSIVE\" if empty_side else \"CLEAN\")\n    out = {\"files_before\": len(before), \"files_after\": len(after),\n           \"added\": added, \"removed\": removed, \"changed\": changed,\n           \"mutated\": mutated,\n           # `mutated_known` is False whenever a side is empty: even a measured removal leaves the\n           # resulting subject's state unmeasurable, so the field never vouches for an empty side.\n           # `mutated` itself stays honest about the measured removed/changed difference.\n           \"mutated_known\": not empty_side,\n           \"inconclusive\": empty_side,\n           \"verdict\": verdict,\n           \"rule\": \"removed/changed => verdict void; added is recorded, not fatal; \"\n                   \"an empty side => INCONCLUSIVE (never a clean verdict from nothing)\"}\n    if empty_side:\n        out[\"inconclusive_reason\"] = (\"a snapshot side is empty, so mutation cannot be measured; \"\n                                      \"this comparison is INCONCLUSIVE, not clean\")\n    return out\n\n\ndef _is_dispatchable(entry: dict) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"C2: does this arm's own declaration permit a spawn?\n\n    NOT dispatchable when the artifact says so (`dispatchable is False`) or when the declared\n    `feasibility` string starts with UNREACHABLE / CONFIG_COMPLETE_NOT_ACTIVATED. The assigned provider/model pair, the declared transport_model\n    and every non-default alternate are selectable; an alternate is owner-listed and never auto-selected,\n    so naming one with --require-arm is explicit operator selection rather than a violation. An arm that\n    appears nowhere in the file is NOT an assignment, whatever the pool says -- a pool edit must not be\n    equivalent to a routing decision.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = json.loads(ASSIGNMENT_FILE.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a raising gate must become a typed refusal\n        return {\"ok\": False, \"arms\": [], \"revision\": None,\n                \"detail\": f\"cannot read {ASSIGNMENT_FILE}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\"}\n    want = str(role).upper()\n    for row in data.get(\"assignment\") or []:\n        label = row.get(\"role\") or row.get(\"lane_group\")\n        if str(label or \"\").upper() != want:\n            continue\n        arms = []\n        prov, model = row.get(\"assigned_provider\"), row.get(\"assigned_model\")\n        if prov and model:\n            arms.append(f\"{prov}/{model}\")\n        if row.get(\"transport_model\"):\n            arms.append(str(row[\"transport_model\"]))\n        primary = list(arms)                      # what the owner assigned -- the decision\n        fallback = []\n        for alt in row.get(\"non_default_alternates\") or []:\n            if alt.get(\"provider\") and alt.get(\"model\"):\n                fallback.append(f\"{alt['provider']}/{alt['model']}\")\n        # FAR A4 / TT-R20-6: a declared fallback chain must be REPRESENTABLE, otherwise strict selection\n        # has no lawful way to degrade and an operator either edits the pool (silently rewriting the\n        # assignment) or disables strictness. Accept `fallbacks` as provider/model strings or objects.\n        for fb in row.get(\"fallbacks\") or []:\n            if isinstance(fb, dict) and fb.get(\"provider\") and fb.get(\"model\"):\n                fallback.append(f\"{fb['provider']}/{fb['model']}\")\n            elif isinstance(fb, str) and \"/\" in fb:\n                fallback.append(fb.strip())\n        arms.extend(fallback)\n        declared_fallbacks = [str(fb.get(\"provider\")) + \"/\" + str(fb.get(\"model\"))\n                              if isinstance(fb, dict) and fb.get(\"provider\") and fb.get(\"model\")\n                              else (fb.strip() if isinstance(fb, str) else None)\n                              for fb in (row.get(\"fallbacks\") or [])]\n        declared_fallbacks = [f for f in declared_fallbacks if f]\n        return {\"ok\": True, \"arms\": sorted(set(arms)), \"revision\": data.get(\"revision\"),\n                \"role\": label, \"authority\": row.get(\"authority\"),\n                \"shape\": \"role\" if row.get(\"role\") else \"lane_group\",\n                \"primary_arms\": sorted(set(primary)),\n                \"fallback_arms\": sorted(set(fallback)),\n                \"declared_fallbacks\": sorted(set(declared_fallbacks)),\n                \"fallbacks_declared\": bool(declared_fallbacks),\n                \"alternates_are_operator_selectable\": bool(row.get(\"non_default_alternates\") or declared_fallbacks)}\n    return {\"ok\": False, \"arms\": [], \"revision\": data.get(\"revision\"),\n            \"detail\": f\"no assignment row for role {role!r} in {ASSIGNMENT_FILE}\"}\n\n\ndef home_isolation_fields(args) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"FIX 3/4 (C3): the resolved-home verdict EVERY receipt carries, computed from one place.\n\n    The oracle requires every receipt -- including every refusal path -- to record the resolved Codex\n    home and whether it is the declared lane home. The value is RESOLVED (FIX 4) so a relative\n    `--codex-home .` is recorded as an absolute path, and the same resolved form feeds the comparison\n    against the declared lane home.\n    \"\"\"\n    resolved = Path(args.codex_home).resolve()\n    iso = lane_home_isolation(resolved, DEFAULT_LANE_HOME)\n    return {\"codex_home\": str(resolved),\n            \"codex_home_is_declared_lane_home\": iso[\"codex_home_is_declared_lane_home\"]}\n\n\ndef nonce_is_blank(nonce) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"FIX 5: an absent or whitespace-only nonce certifies nothing -- the substring test in\n    nonce_in_agent_message would be vacuously true, so `--nonce \"\"` must be REFUSED, not treated as\n    \"the check is disabled\".\"\"\"\n    return not isinstance(nonce, str) or nonce.strip() == \"\"\n\n\ndef served_arm_of(provider, model):\n    \"\"\"Compose the served arm identity from what actually answered.\n\n    REC07 G1: the relay providers report the model as a provider-qualified slug\n    (``opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash``) while the OAuth arm reports a bare name\n    (``gpt-6.1-sol``).  Prefixing unconditionally doubled the slug, so a run that really served the\n    required arm was recorded as ERR_REQUIRED_ARM_NOT_SERVED.\n\n    Prefix only when the model does not already carry its own provider, and never strip: a model\n    qualified by a DIFFERENT provider still composes to an arm that cannot equal the requirement.\n\n    Returns None when no qualified identity can be asserted (missing provider, missing model, or a\n    degenerate provider == model).  A None served arm must be reported as unqualified rather than\n    compared as if it were a model-only identity: a bare ``--require-arm`` would otherwise be\n    satisfied with no provider evidence at all (challenge V04, item 6).\n    \"\"\"\n    provider = str(provider or \"\").strip()\n    model = str(model or \"\").strip()\n    if not provider or not model or model == provider:\n        return None\n    if model.startswith(provider + \"/\"):\n        return model\n    return f\"{provider}/{model}\"\n\n\n# --- Owner-declared provider ALIASES (FAR-B1-v2, N-6) ------------------------------------------\n# Some arms ANSWER under a different provider label than the one the lane bound: the OpenAI-OAuth\n# turn is served through the desktop router, which reports provider=desktop_router, while the pool\n# declares provider=openai-codex, and both are billed to the SAME owner OAuth credential. Before this\n# table the run was recorded as ERR_REQUIRED_ARM_NOT_SERVED while usage_receipt_ok was true.\n#\n# This is a DECLARATION of one measured equivalence, not a relaxation of the guard:\n#   * only pairs listed here are canonicalised -- an undeclared provider still fails closed;\n#   * the payer is recorded with the alias, so the equivalence is \"same credential\", never \"same name\";\n#   * both the RAW and the CANONICAL served arm go on the receipt, so a reader can always see what\n#     actually answered and whether an alias was applied to make the comparison pass.\nARM_ALIASES = {\n    (\"desktop_router\", \"gpt-6.1-sol\"): {\n        \"arm\": \"openai-codex/gpt-6.1-sol\",\n        \"payer\": \"owner-openai-oauth\",\n        \"declared_by\": \"OWNER (FAR-B1-v2 N-6)\",\n        \"basis\": \"desktop router proxies the owner's OpenAI OAuth session; pool arm is openai-codex\",\n    },\n}\n\n\ndef canonical_arm(provider, model):\n    \"\"\"Compose the served arm, then apply a DECLARED alias if one matches exactly.\n\n    Returns ``(canonical_arm, alias_record_or_None)``. ``alias_record`` is None when no alias applied,\n    so callers can prove that a passing comparison did not depend on an undeclared equivalence.\n    \"\"\"\n    raw = served_arm_of(provider, model)\n    if raw is None:\n        return None, None\n    rec = ARM_ALIASES.get((str(provider or \"\").strip(), str(model or \"\").strip()))\n    if rec and rec.get(\"arm\"):\n        return str(rec[\"arm\"]), dict(rec, raw=raw)\n    return raw, None\n\n\ndef failure_disposition(attempts: list) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"C1: the failure code for a run that produced no usable turn, without conflating conditions.\n\n    Kept additive: a run that answered (exit 0 + a valid usage receipt) but emitted no nonce reads\n    ERR_NO_ARM_EMITTED_NONCE. EVERY case where no arm produced an acceptable turn -- including a run\n    where every attempt timed out -- keeps the generic ERR_LANE_ALL_ROUTES_EXHAUSTED, per the frozen\n    oracle. The timeout CASE is still MEASURED via `timed_out_arms` (an additive field), but the CODE\n    is never ERR_ALL_ATTEMPTS_TIMED_OUT (that constant/branch is removed, not left dead). Returns\n    receipt fields to merge.\n\n    I3 (pass-5): the FROZEN oracle's C1 classification TAKES PRECEDENCE. A turn with exit 0, a valid\n    usage receipt and no nonce is ERR_NO_ARM_EMITTED_NONCE + nonce_absent_arms whatever the carrier\n    shape was -- an invalid carrier merely means the TEXT is absent, which is exactly \"emitted no\n    nonce\".
